Awesome Farm is a cosy co-op island game for up to sixteen friends. Everybody starts on their own little island far out at sea, chops, mines and farms, buys land until the islands join up, and then builds one big farm together: machines, creatures that work, bosses, expeditions into the rifts and a long story. You can play alone in your browser too.

Search for anything above (press /), or open a section from the contents. Every name is a link.

181 items122 recipes69 buildings111 skills16 creatures12 monsters6 bosses16 chapters49 side quests67 medals

Start here

You wake up on a small island with a flint pick and a wooden club. Everything else you gather, craft or build yourself. The tutorial card at the top of the screen walks you through the first ten minutes; the steps are listed below, followed by the in-game guide's first page.

The game saves by itself. In a solo world time stops while a window is open; online, the world keeps going.

The first ten minutes (16 tutorial steps)

  1. 1. Take a walk
    Use WASD to walk around your island. The + and − keys zoom in and out.
    On a phone: Touch and drag on the left to walk around. Pinch with two fingers to zoom.
  2. 2. Gather wood
    Hold Space next to a tree until it falls. The wood flies into your pockets. Get a few!
    On a phone: Hold ACT next to a tree until it falls. The wood flies into your pockets. Get a few!
  3. 3. Peek in your Backpack
    Press I to open your Backpack. Everything you pick up lives in there.
    On a phone: Tap BAG to open your Backpack. Everything you pick up lives in there.
  4. 4. Build a Workbench
    Press B for the Build menu, open its Crafting tab and pick the Workbench (6 wood). Then put it down on free ground.
    On a phone: Tap BUILD for the Build menu, open its Crafting tab and pick the Workbench (6 wood). Then put it down on free ground.
  5. 5. Saw a plank
    Your Workbench is out of sight. Walk back to it, then press E.
    On a phone: Your Workbench is out of sight. Walk back to it, then press USE.
  6. 6. Break a boulder
    Hold Space next to a grey boulder. Stone builds your Campfire and Market Stall.
    On a phone: Hold ACT next to a grey boulder. Stone builds your Campfire and Market Stall.
  7. 7. Build a Chest
    Press B for the Build menu, open its Storage tab and pick the Chest (8 wood). Then put something inside.
    On a phone: Tap BUILD for the Build menu, open its Storage tab and pick the Chest (8 wood). Then put something inside.
  8. 8. Plant a seed
    Press B for the Build menu, open its Farming tab and pick the Garden Bed (3 wood). Crops grow in it.
    On a phone: Tap BUILD for the Build menu, open its Farming tab and pick the Garden Bed (3 wood). Crops grow in it.
  9. 9. Watch it grow
    Crops grow by themselves, a little faster in the rain. Press E when it looks full-grown.
    On a phone: Crops grow by themselves, a little faster in the rain. Tap USE when it looks full-grown.
  10. 10. Sell something
    Press B for the Build menu, open its Special tab and pick the Market Stall (6 wood + 4 stone). Then sell things at it.
    On a phone: Tap BUILD for the Build menu, open its Special tab and pick the Market Stall (6 wood + 4 stone). Then sell things at it.
  11. 11. Buy new land
    Walk to the shore. Pale squares out at sea are for sale: face one and press E.
    On a phone: Walk to the shore. Pale squares out at sea are for sale: face one and tap USE.
  12. 12. Light a Campfire
    Monsters come out at night. Press B, open Light and place a Campfire (5 wood + 2 stone).
    On a phone: Monsters come out at night. Tap BUILD, open Light and place a Campfire (5 wood + 2 stone).
  13. 13. Have a snack
    Swinging uses energy (the yellow bar). Press F to eat and fill it up.
    On a phone: Swinging uses energy (the yellow bar). Tap EAT to eat and fill it up.
  14. 14. Learn a skill
    You have a skill point! Press K, then click a glowing skill to learn it.
    On a phone: You have a skill point! Tap MENU, then Skills, and tap a glowing skill.
  15. 15. Pin it to your hotbar
    Open your Backpack (I), then right-click an item to pin it to the hotbar. Keys 1 to 8 pick a slot.
    On a phone: Open BAG, tap a hotbar slot, then tap an item to pin it there.
  16. 16. Survive the night
    Stay by your Campfire and hit monsters with Space. Make it to sunrise!
    On a phone: Stay by your Campfire and hit monsters with ACT. Make it to sunrise!

The loop of the whole game

  1. Use WASD to walk. Hold Space (or the mouse button) beside a tree, rock or berry bush to harvest it. What drops flies into your pockets.
  2. Press B (Build) and place a Workbench (6 wood). Chests, campfires and garden beds are in the same menu. Press C for Crafting: rope and bait by hand, most other things at a station.
  3. The quest tracker at the top left always shows your next steps. Hover a line to see how to do it; press J for the whole Journal.
  4. Every level gives a skill point. Press K and spend it: tools, recipes and buildings unlock through the skill tree.
  5. When the sun sets, monsters come out. Light a campfire and stay near it, or fight back with your Wooden Club (hotbar slot 2).
  6. Walk to the edge of your island and press E to buy land. Keep going and your island joins up with your friends’ islands.
  7. In the last minute of the day, gather at a Campfire or Table with a friend, or with your companion beside you. A ring fills over the fire, and then you all have Hearthside until dawn: hearts and energy mend faster, +15% XP.
  • The game saves by itself. In a solo world, time stops while a window is open.
  • Forgot a key? Esc opens the menu, and its Controls tab lists every key.

Your first hour, chapter by chapter

The Journal's story picks up where the tutorial ends. The first chapters, in order (the full list is in Quests):

  1. Fresh Soil: Chop 8 trees, Build a Workbench, Craft 4 planks, Build a Chest, Reach level 3.
  2. A Place to Rest: Build a Campfire, Build a Garden Bed, Harvest 3 crops, Buy a new plot of land, Survive a night.
  3. Hot Metal: Build a Furnace, Smelt 6 iron bars, Learn 3 skills (K), Reach level 6, Break 8 boulders.
  4. Reaching Out: Own 6 plots, Build a Market Stall, Earn 150 coins selling goods, Find another farmer: join your islands together, Catch 3 fish (a rod is one workbench away).
  5. Night Watch: Defeat 12 monsters, Wear body armor, Survive 3 nights, Build 2 lanterns, Reach level 10.

Controls

Movement and the fishing key follow the position of the keys, not the letters printed on them. On an AZERTY keyboard you walk with the keys where WASD would be, and the game names them for you everywhere (in the guide, the tutorial and the hints). The number keys work unshifted on AZERTY too.

On a phone there are buttons instead of keys: a stick on the lower left, and round buttons on the right.

On QWERTY you walk with W A S D and fish with Q; on AZERTY the same keys are Z Q S D and A. The game picks your layout by itself (Settings lets you choose).

Keyboard

Progression

Almost everything you do earns XP. Every level gives one skill point to spend in the skill tree, and story chapters, bosses, expeditions, bounties, goals and medals pay extra points.

The level cap is 80. All 111 skill nodes at every rank cost 411 points in all, so choose: you will not have them all from levels alone.

Every wave you clear pays XP too, more on higher tiers and later waves.

Everything else

  • Crafting: every recipe lists its XP in Crafting. Machines pay their recipe's XP to whoever owns them while they are online.
  • Building anything: a few XP, more for buildings that take more kinds of material.
  • Buying land, planting seeds, fishing (more for rare and big fish, and pearls), befriending and hatching creatures (more for rarer ones), digging in the caves.
  • Bounties pay XP worth half their coins; side quests pay the XP below; Creatures teach their keeper: whenever one of your creatures earns XP at a job (a den, an island post, a machine or a workshop), you earn a share of it too, online or not. Dawn tells you how much.

Every XP boost

Boosts add up: two +50% boosts make +100%.

SourceBoost
Skill Shared Lessons (Taming)+10% XP from working creatures per rank, 3 ranks
Skill Pack Lore (Taming)+25% XP from working creatures per rank, 1 rank
Skill Scholar (Explorer)+6% XP gain per rank, 5 ranks
Skill Wisdom (Explorer)+6% XP gain per rank, 4 ranks
Skill Sage (Explorer)+10% XP gain per rank, 3 ranks
Skill Wanderer (Explorer)+10% XP gain per rank, 1 rank
Buff Studious+50% XP gain: Scholar's Tea (5 min)
Buff Insight+100% XP gain: Elixir of Insight (4 min)
Buff Kinship+25% XP from working creatures: Kinship Tonic (5 min)
Buff Scholar's Day+100% XP gain: Scholar's Day: everybody gets it from dawn to the next dawn.
Buff Hearthside+15% XP gain: The evening hearth: sit at a fire with company in the last minute of the day.
Season wish Bright Minds+20% XP gain for the whole season
Expedition boon Quick Study+50% XP gain for the run
Bottled Memories25% of what your current level needs, at once (XP boosts count too)
Creatures teach their keeper25% of the XP your creatures earn at work comes to you too, plus every "XP from working creatures" bonus above

Creatures learning faster

SourceBoost
Skill Fast Learners+10% Creature XP per rank, 4 ranks
Skill Good Provider+5% Creature XP per rank, 3 ranks
Skill Nursery+5% Creature XP per rank, 3 ranks
Skill Prime Training+20% Creature XP per rank, 3 ranks
Skill Pack Lore+10% Creature XP per rank, 1 rank
Skill Spirit Bond+30% Creature XP per rank, 1 rank
Buff Kinship+50% Creature XP
Season wish Kindred Spirits+25% Creature XP
Trait Bright+25% experience

Scholar's Day: double XP for everybody from dawn to dawn. It falls on day five and every tenth day after it, and now and then besides. The weather vane shows it coming, and XP brews stack with it.

What unlocks what

Recipes and buildings with a padlock are opened by a skill. Each skill node needs at least one rank in ANY of the nodes it lists, starting from the hub. The path shown is the cheapest one in skill points from the hub.

A recipe also needs its station: the Anvil needs Blacksmithing, the Kitchen Kitchen Know-How, and so on (see Buildings). Menus only list a station once you can use it, and the Build menu hides Logistics and Power until the skill tree opens them.

Skill tree

Six branches fan out from the hub. A node can be learned once any one of its listed nodes has a rank. Bonuses are per rank and add up; unlocks come with the first rank. Keystones are the big nodes at the end of a branch.

Gathering (18 nodes, 69 points)

Hit harder, harvest more, waste nothing.

Items (181)

Every item in the game. Click a name anywhere on this page to jump to it. "How to get it" lists every source; "Used for" lists every recipe and building that needs it. You can carry up to the base stack of each item (packs and skills raise it); gear stacks to nine.

Swatches: the colour is the kind of item, the ring its rarity (Common Uncommon Rare Epic Legendary).

Materials (46)

Wood

Common Material · sells for 1 coin
Fuel
14 s of burn time

Sturdy logs. The start of every farm.

How to get it
  • Breaking a Tree (2-3) in Meadow, Quarry, Goldsand, Snowcap, Bog.
  • Breaking a Great Oak (36-48).
  • Loot crates, Common class (6-12 before the crate's bonus): Wooden Crate, Silver Crate, Golden Crate.

Stone

Common Material · sells for 1 coin

Grey and heavy. Builds almost everything.

How to get it
  • Breaking a Boulder (2-3) in Meadow, Quarry, Goldsand, Snowcap, Bog.
  • Breaking an Iron vein (1) in Quarry, Snowcap.
  • Breaking a Copper vein (1) in Quarry, Goldsand, Snowcap.
  • Breaking a Crystal cluster (1) in Snowcap, Bog.
  • Breaking a Titan Boulder (36-48).
  • Digging rock in the caves.
  • Dropped by Rockling (2-4, 90% chance).
  • Dropped by Stone Colossus (12-20).
  • Loot crates, Common class (6-12 before the crate's bonus): Wooden Crate, Silver Crate, Golden Crate.
  • Side quest reward: Down the Shaft (20).

Coal

Common Material · sells for 2 coins
Fuel
50 s of burn time

Burns hot and long. Fuel for furnaces.

How to get it
  • Breaking a Boulder (1, 25% chance) in Meadow, Quarry, Goldsand, Snowcap, Bog.
  • Breaking a Coal seam (2-3) in Quarry, Snowcap.
  • Breaking a Titan Boulder (4-8, 60% chance).
  • Digging ore veins in the caves.
  • Dropped by Skeleton (1, 30% chance).
  • Loot crates, Common class (4-8 before the crate's bonus): Wooden Crate, Silver Crate, Golden Crate.
Used for
  • Smoked Trout at the Kitchen.
  • Steel at the Furnace.
  • Fuel: burns for 50 s in a furnace or generator.

Iron Ore

Rusty rock with a metal heart. Smelt it in a furnace.

How to get it

  • Digging ore veins in the caves.

  • Dropped by Stone Colossus (6-10).
Used for
  • Iron Bar at the Furnace.

Hums faintly when you hold it. Needs a gold pick to mine.

How to get it
  • Breaking a Wildflower (1, 20% chance) in Meadow, Quarry, Goldsand.
  • Breaking a Wild cotton (1, 25% chance) in Meadow.
  • Made at the Workbench: 2 from 1 Cotton.
  • The travelling trader (from day 2, 4-10 at a time).
  • Postcards from the island folk, now and then.
Used for
  • Plant it in a Garden Bed to grow Cotton.

  • Made at the Rift Forge: from 20 Rift Shard, 4 Crystal Shard, 10 Cloth, 10 Rope.

Taming Pod

Common Other · sells for 4 coins

Throw it at a wild creature (T) to befriend it.

How to get it
  • Made at the Workbench: 2 from 2 Plank, 3 Plant Fiber, 1 Slime Gel (learn Pod Crafter (Taming)).
  • Made at the Assembler: 2 from 2 Plank, 3 Plant Fiber, 1 Slime Gel in 3 s (learn Pod Crafter (Taming)).
  • Loot crates, Uncommon class (2-3 before the crate's bonus): Wooden Crate, Silver Crate, Golden Crate, Mythic Crate.
  • Clearing the Mossy Rift (1-2, 30% chance).
  • The travelling trader (from day 1, 4-10 at a time).
  • Story reward: Wild Hearts (4).
  • Side quest reward: A Friend in Need (4).
  • A gift your companion digs up (nice find, 1-2).
  • The Fortune Wheel's Pods wedge (3).
Used for
  • Throw it at a wild creature (T) to befriend it: catch power x1.

Wooden Crate

Uncommon Other · sells for 12 coins
Opens
Click it in the Backpack: see Wooden Crate

A battered crate that rattles when you shake it. Click it in your backpack to open it.

How to get it
  • Monsters of tier 0: 1.5% chance, doubled for elites.
  • Monsters of tier 1 (or an elite one tier lower): 3% chance, doubled for elites.
  • Monsters of tier 2 (or an elite one tier lower): 6% chance, doubled for elites.
  • Felling a Titan node with friends (45% base chance per helper).
  • Lucky Finds and golden nodes now and then, and buried treasure.
  • Side quest reward: A Light in the Garden (1).
  • Side quest reward: A Spoonful of Luck (2).
  • Side quest reward: Land Rush (2).
  • Side quest reward: Friends of the Light (1).
  • Side quest reward: Odd Glass (2).
  • Side quest reward: Keep the Lights On (1).
  • Side quest reward: A Spin of Fortune (1).
  • Side quest reward: Press Your Luck (2).
  • Side quest reward: Lucky Streak (3).
  • Side quest reward: Dig Deeper (1).
  • Side quest reward: A Letter from the Sea (2).
  • A gift your companion digs up (rare find, 1).
  • The Fortune Wheel's Crate wedge.

Message in a Bottle

Uncommon Other · sells for 15 coins
Opens
A letter from long ago (and it shows you a buried treasure)

Somebody sealed a letter in this a long time ago. Click it to read it.

How to get it
  • Loot crates, Uncommon class (1 before the crate's bonus): Wooden Crate, Silver Crate, Golden Crate, Mythic Crate.
  • Fishing: a 6% chance per catch that a bottle came up too.

Crafting (122 recipes)

Stations craft instantly: stand next to one and press C (or E on it). Crafting also takes from the chests within eight tiles of you, pockets first. Machines (Furnace, Sawmill, Millstone, Assembler) take ingredients and work on their own over time.

By hand (2)

No station needed: press C anywhere.

Buildings (69)

Press B for the Build menu. Most buildings are placed once; walls, floors, belts, beds, chests and fences keep being placed until you stop. Holding X takes down what you point at and gives back part of its materials.

Farming

Garden beds grow crops in two stages. You can walk right over a bed. Rain makes growing beds grow faster (it does not water them), the season changes how fast crops grow, and autumn harvests often give one more.

Build a Garden Bed (3 Wood), stand by it with a seed and press E to plant; press E again when it is ripe. Rain makes growing beds grow up to 50% faster. A companion with the Farm task, or a creature on a Farming post, harvests, replants and waters for you.

Crops

Time to ripe with no skills, in each season. Value is an average summer harvest at the market, and per minute of bed time.

SeedCropSpringSummerAutumnWinterYieldSeed backXPValue
Wheat SeedsWheat11s14s14s31s2-350%35 (21.4/min)
Carrot SeedsCarrot16s20s20s44s2-345%47.5 (22.5/min)
Pumpkin SeedsPumpkin22s28s28s1:021-250%69 (19.3/min)
Cotton SeedsCotton19s24s24s53s2-445%56 (15/min)
Beet SeedsBeet13s16s16s36s2-350%37.5 (28.1/min)
Corn SeedsCorn16s20s20s44s2-445%49 (27/min)
Pepper SeedsChili Pepper18s22s22s49s2-345%510 (27.3/min)
Flax SeedsFlax19s24s24s53s3-545%58 (20/min)
Melon SeedsMelon26s32s32s1:111-250%812 (22.5/min)

Seasons

A season lasts 7 days; a year is 4 seasons.

SeasonCrop growthExtra crop chanceRainNight lengthIn words
Springx1.25-x1.5x1Crops grow fast and the rain comes more often.
Summerx1-x0.7x0.8Long, warm days and short nights.
Autumnx140%x1.1x1.05Harvest time: crops often give one more.
Winterx0.45-x1x1.3Crops barely grow and the nights are long. Keep a fire lit.

Factory and automation

Drills mine the ore veins under them, belts carry items, inserters move them between chests, belts and machines, and power poles wire machines to generators. Every machine says what it is doing: hover it, long-press it on a phone, or press E on it. Press L for the Factory view.

Factory view key: L. Badges over machines: green gear working, yellow hourglass waiting for ingredients, red bolt no power, red flame no fuel, red cross blocked.

Power

A Power Pole wires up machines within 3 tiles and links to poles within 7; poles that reach each other form one grid. When a grid makes less than its machines want, they all slow down. Wind comes and goes (36% to 100% of a turbine's top output).

BuildingMakesUsesStoresBurns fuel
Inserter3

Rates and reach

Drill
one item every 3.5 s with all four tiles on ore (slower with fewer); Drill Bits and Full Automation add more
Inserter
one swing every 0.8 s
Tunnel
entrance and exit up to 6 tiles apart

What a working creature says

StatusWhat to do
WorkingAll good.
Nothing to doThere is nothing left for it to do here right now. It keeps an eye out.
Needs a chestBuild a Chest on the island (or next to the machine, within nine tiles) so it has somewhere to put things.
Out of suppliesPut what it needs in a Chest nearby: ore, wood, ingredients.
Out of fuelPut wood, peat or coal in a Chest next to the machine.
No powerConnect the machine to power.
Pick a recipeOpen the Assembler and choose what it should build.
Waiting for ordersOpen the workshop (E), pick a recipe and give it an order.
Order finishedIt finished the whole order. Give it another.
All tidyEverything is where it belongs. It will tidy up again when something changes.

World

The world is a big grid of island plots in open sea. Buying a plot next to land you own raises it from the sea. Plots have a biome and sometimes a modifier. The plot in the very middle is the Old Heart's.

The world is 35 × 35 plots of 18 × 18 tiles. Up to 16 farmers each get a home island; the plots from the middle out come in rings of biomes, and far out are the wilds. A day is 5 min of daylight and 50 s of night; a warning comes 60 s before dusk and an alarm 20 s before.

Biomes

Land price multiplier, how often each resource grows, and the monsters that roam there at the highest threat level.

Titan nodes

With at least two farmers on the world, a tree or boulder on owned land now and then grows into a Titan. It only takes damage while two or more different farmers have swung at it recently. Everybody who helped gets their own drops (more for every extra pair of hands), the XP and a crate roll.

Hands
2 or more farmers within 3 s
Bonus
+50% for every extra hand, up to 4 hands
Grows
2% of new trees and boulders on owned land, at most 1 per island, once 2 farmers have a farm

Buying land

Land is bought at the shore: face a pale square next to land you own and press E. The price grows with every plot YOU have bought (until it starts growing by a fixed step), and is multiplied by the plot's biome and modifier.

The Old Heart

The plot in the exact middle of the map (plot 17, 17) belongs to The Old Heart. Buy land towards it, build a Boss Altar there and wake it with the Heart Sigil.

The Dread Reaches

Four blocks of haunted land sit out in the wilds on the diagonals. Nobody can buy them, but the land beside them can be bought, and you can walk in. The dead haunt them day and night while a farmer is inside, and a warden (one of the altar bosses, much tougher) sleeps at the heart of each. Everything there is richer: more ore, a treasure chest in every outer plot and a vault by the warden.

Blocks
4, each 3 × 3 plots
Haunting
4 monsters as soon as somebody walks in, 2 more per farmer inside, at most 16; one tier tougher than your level calls for
Who haunts
Night Bat, Bog Wisp, Skeleton, Hollow Knight, Wraith
Warden
an altar boss with 2 times the health and 1.2 times the damage; wakes again 5 days after it falls
Spoils
the ordinary boss spoils plus gold crates, rift shards and a chance at a mythic crate

The caves

A Mine Shaft leads down to the caves, a second map as big as the world that lines up with it. Hold the action key beside rock to dig; everything you dig stays dug. Ore grows in clusters that get richer towards the edge of the map. Creatures of the dark come for whoever is digging, and ancient chambers far out hold a vault guarded by elites until it is opened.

Getting there
learn Mine Shafts and build a Mine Shaft
Ore by distance from the middle
DistanceOre clusters
0% to 35%Coal 36%, Iron Ore 34%, Copper Ore 30%
35% to 70%Coal 12%, Iron Ore 28%, Copper Ore 22%, Gold Ore 30%, Crystal Shard 8%
70% to the edgeCoal 8%, Iron Ore 14%, Copper Ore 10%, Gold Ore 40%, Crystal Shard 28%
Crystal
needs a tier 2 pick (Golden Pick or better)
Ancient chambers
16, at least a third of the way out
What lives there
Slime, Skeleton, Night Bat, Scarab, Rockling, Bone Archer, Bog Wisp, Hollow Knight, Wraith; deeper means nastier. 3 to 8 come for whoever digs.

Weather and nights

Weather is decided by the world's seed and the day, so everybody sees the same sky. Some days it rains (sometimes a storm with lightning), some mornings are foggy. A Weather Vane forecasts the next three days.

NightWhat happensWhen
Blood MoonMore monsters, stronger monsters, and twice the spoils. Survive until dawn.Every seventh night. More monsters, more elites, more XP and double coins from kills, and a coin reward at dawn for everybody still standing.
Meteor ShowerStars are falling onto your land. Ore and crystal will be waiting at dawn.Now and then. Crystal, gold and iron nodes fall onto owned land during the night.
Fairy NightEveryone is healed and feeling lucky. The wild folk are out in numbers.Now and then. Everybody online is healed, filled with energy and made Lucky, and more wild creatures are about.

Scholar's Day: Everything anybody does teaches them twice as much, until dawn. Scholar's Day: double XP for everybody from dawn to dawn. It falls on day five and every tenth day after it, and now and then besides. The weather vane shows it coming, and XP brews stack with it.

Season wishes (14)

On the first morning of every season after the first, the farm is offered three wishes (the same three for everybody, from the world's seed). Everybody online votes; the winner blesses every farmer until the season ends.

3 are offered each season.

Good FishingThe fish bite sooner and are easier to land.+30% Bite speed +15% Fishing luck

Combat

Monsters come out at night, and they are tuned to your level, not to the age of the world: a new farmer in an old world meets the same nights a new world would give. Bosses are summoned at the Boss Altar with a sigil.

How hard the night is

Your threat level is your own level, or the average level of the farmers playing near you (a party shares one difficulty). It decides which tiers of monster roam, how many come each night, whether elites appear and how hard newcomers get bitten.

Threat levelHighest monster tierMonsters per farmer each nightDamage you takeElite chance

30314100%22%
40314100%22%
60314100%22%
80314100%22%

Farmers within 800 px of each other count as one party. Monsters never appear within 80 px of a farmer, nor on a floor or inside a closed room. A campfire keeps them 44 px away and heals a heart every 12 s at night.

Elites

Elites are tougher versions of ordinary monsters: more health, harder hits, more XP, double coins and a better chance of a crate, and now and then a potion or a bar on top. They start to show up once your threat level is high enough, and a Blood Moon makes them far more common.

Bosses (6)

Learn Boss Hunter (Combat) to build the Boss Altar. Craft a sigil at the altar from the drops of that biome's monsters, then use the sigil at the altar to summon the boss. Its health scales with the farmers nearby, it stays leashed to its arena, and everybody who helped is paid. Red marks on the ground show where an attack will land. The Old Heart can only be woken at an altar on the centre plot.

Some boss patterns only appear while two or more farmers are up in the arena. A lone farmer gets an ordinary pattern in its place (shown as "alone:" below).

PatternWhat it does
slamSlams the ground around itself: step out of the red ring.
summonCalls its minions.
radialFires a ring of shots in every direction.
aimedFires a volley straight at a farmer.
chargeDashes through the arena in a line.
rainMarked spots on the ground get hit from above.
spiralSpins out a spiral of shots.
sweepA wide sweep in front of it.
leapLeaps onto a marked spot.
freezeCo-op only: freezes a farmer solid until a friend thaws them.
hexCo-op only: curses a farmer; touch a friend to pass the curse on.
chainCo-op only: chains two farmers together.

Co-op statuses

StatusWhat happens
FrozenFrozen solid: you cannot move, swing or dash, and nothing can hurt you. A friend who stands next to you and holds E thaws you in 1.5 seconds; otherwise you thaw by yourself in 8.
HexedA curse that hurts a little more every second and fades after 12 seconds. Stand next to a friend and it jumps to them.
ChainedChained to a friend for 16 seconds. Further than 7 tiles apart, the chain pulls and hurts you both.

Going down, and the lost backpack

When your hearts run out you are down. A friend can hold E beside you to pick you up, and you lose nothing. If nobody comes (or you press R), you wake up at home: you lose part of the XP you had towards your next level and your whole backpack stays where you fell as a Lost Backpack. Anybody can take things out of it; it glows at night. Coins and what you wear are safe, and falling on an expedition costs nothing.

Waiting for a friend
25 s (6 s when nobody can come)
Reviving
hold E beside them for 2.5 s
Waking at home
costs 50% of the XP you had towards the next level
Lost Backpack
lies where you fell for 120 min (about 21 days)

Tip: carry a stack of food and a few healing potions, and build a campfire wherever you plan to spend the night.

Dash and the Perfect dash

With the Dash skill, Shift (DASH on a phone) rolls you away with a moment of invulnerability. Dash through an attack at the last instant and you pull off a Perfect dash: the dash's energy comes back, time slows for a beat, and your next hit on a monster is a critical one.

Unlock
learn Dash
Energy
6 per dash (a Perfect dash gives it back)
Invulnerable
0.45 s
Cooldown
1.1 s
Perfect
Perfect for 4 s: +100% Critical chance

Creatures (16 species)

Wild creatures wander owned land, rarely and one at a time. Throw a pod (T) to befriend one. Tamed creatures level up, can follow you as a companion, work beside you, or take a standing job on an island, at a machine or at a workshop.

Catching

Learn Pod Crafter (Taming) to craft Taming Pods. Hurt creatures are easier to catch. Better pods and the Taming skills raise the odds. The chances below are for an unhurt creature with no bonuses.

Pods
Taming Pod x1 (learn Pod Crafter (Taming)), Greater Pod x1.7 (learn Beast Whisperer (Taming)), Master Pod x2.6 (learn Rare Finder (Taming))
Wild ones
at most 1 near a farmer at a time (4 on a Fairy Night); a pod reaches 118 px
Rare ones
Rare Finder makes rare creatures show up more

Jobs, posts, dens and workshops

A creature can only do a job it has aptitude for (1 to 5 dots); more dots work faster. Island jobs and workshop jobs are standing posts: they keep working while you are away.

You can have 4 creatures at work at once, more with Den Keeper (+1 per rank), Stable Master (+1 per rank), Beastmaster (+2 per rank). A Creature Den houses 3 that work the land around it. Island workers carry what they get to a chest near the middle of the island; machine keepers fetch from and fill the chests within nine tiles.

JobIn shortBeside you (field task)On an island (post)Runs
LumberFells trees and gathers plants near the den.Chops trees and picks plants near you. What it gets goes straight into your pockets.Fells the trees and picks the plants on the island, and carries them to a chest there.-
MiningBreaks rocks and ore near the den.Breaks rocks and ore near you (what its strength allows). The goods go into your pockets.Breaks the rocks and ore on the island (what its strength allows) and carries the stone to a chest.-
FarmingWaters beds so they grow faster, and harvests ripe crops.Harvests ripe crops near you, replants from your seeds and waters the rest.Fetches seeds from the chest, plants them in your garden beds, waters them, harvests and carries the crops back to the chest.-
HaulingCarries finished goods from machines, and anything lying about, into storage.-Collects loose goods lying about and the output of machines, and carries it all to a chest.-
SortingTidies the chests: like goes with like.-Walks between the island’s chests and puts like with like: food with food, seeds with seeds, tools with tools. Needs two chests or more.-
KindlingTends furnaces, anvils and kitchens, and speeds up machines nearby.--Anvil, Kitchen, Furnace
HandiworkRuns workbenches, looms, alchemy tables, sawmills, millstones and assemblers.--Workbench, Loom, Alchemy Table, Sawmill, Millstone, Assembler
GuardingDrives off monsters that come near the den.Runs down monsters within a few tiles of you and fights them.Hunts down monsters on the island, day and night, so the others can work in peace.-

Levels and stars

Creatures level up to 30 by working, fighting and treats. Awakening is the late-game way to make a favourite creature stronger: with enough levels and rare goods it earns up to three stars, each giving more health, attack and work speed.

Breeding

Learn Matchmaker (Taming) to build a Hatchery. Two of your creatures and some treats make an egg. The baby is one of the parents' species (now and then a rarer relative of the same element), inherits up to two traits and sometimes grows a new one.

Unlock
learn Matchmaker and build a Hatchery
An egg
3 Creature Treats and 4 min (faster with Nursery)

Companion, petting and gifts

Stand next to your companion and press E to pet it. A few pets a day earn affection. From Fond on, the first pet of each day digs up a gift.

Pets that count
5 a day
Gift odds
from 40 affection: 22% nice find and 3% rare treasure, rising to 34% and 7% at full affection

Friendly10Comes running when you are near.
Fond40Digs up a small gift for you every day.

Expeditions and rifts

An Expedition Dock launches a party to one of the rift islands in the corners of the sea. Each tier is a run of waves with a guardian at the end; between waves everybody picks one of three boons that last only for the run. Falling costs nothing on an expedition.

Learn Expedition Pass (Explorer) and build an Expedition Dock by the water. Up to 4 farmers go together; there are 4 rift islands. You have 22 s to pick a boon. A clear pays coins, XP, rift shards, the tier's loot and a crate; the first clear of a tier also pays skill points. A lost run still pays for the waves you cleared. Once you have cleared a rift you can take omens at the dock for bigger rewards. The rift of the day comes with two omens already on, a bonus and a one-off shard bonus for its first clear. The Abyss has no last wave: a guardian every fifth wave, monsters stronger every wave, and a boon between waves until the pool runs dry. Cash out at the gate whenever you like (or fall) for shards by depth.

Omens (8)

Take up to 3 at the dock once you have cleared a rift; each adds to the coins, XP and shards of the run. The rift of the day has two omens on, +25% on top, and a one-off bonus of shards on its first clear (4 for the Mossy Rift, 7 for the Gravel Rift, 10 for the Hollow Rift, 13 for the Wraith Rift, 16 for the Heart Rift).

SwarmingA third more monsters in every wave.+20%
ChampionsElite monsters are three times as common.+15%
SpartanNo boons between waves.+30%
CursedHealing potions turn to dust.+15%
RelentlessThe next wave arrives almost at once.+10%

Fishing

Face open water with a rod in your pockets and press the fishing key to cast. Wait for the bite (the bobber dips and a gold ! appears), press again to hook it, then answer every tug of a big fish. Missing too many lets it get away; you never lose anything but the fish.

Cast
34 to 124 px out
Tugs
answer each within 0.9 s; 2 slips and it gets away
Bait
Bait: one per cast
Bottles
6% of catches bring up a Message in a Bottle too

Quests

Press J for the Journal. The story runs by itself; side quests are accepted from the Quests tab and count from the moment you accept them.

The story (16 chapters)

A finished chapter pays its reward and opens the next one by itself.

1. Fresh Soil

Chapter 1 of 16

A small island, a flint pick and a lot of trees. Everyone starts here.

  1. Chop 8 trees
    Stand next to a tree and hold Space (or the mouse button). Your pick breaks it and the wood flies to you.
  2. Build a Workbench
    Press B for the build menu, pick the Workbench (6 wood) and click on free ground to place it.
  3. Craft 4 planks
    Stand next to your Workbench, press C, and craft Planks from wood.
  4. Build a Chest
    Press B, pick the Chest (8 wood) and place it. Walk up and press E to put things in; everyone on the farm can use it.
  5. Reach level 3
    Almost everything earns XP: chopping, breaking rocks, crafting, building, fighting. Keep at it.
Reward
40 coins, 1 skill point, 2 Healing Potion

2. A Place to Rest

Chapter 2 of 16

Night brings monsters. A fire, a garden and a bit more land make a home.

  1. Build a Campfire
    Press B, open the Light tab, pick the Campfire (5 wood, 2 stone) and place it. Monsters will not come close, and it heals you at night.
  2. Build a Garden Bed
    Press B, open the Farm tab, pick the Garden Bed (3 wood) and place it.
  3. Harvest 3 crops
    Break wildflowers for seeds, then walk up to your bed and press E to plant. When the plant is fully grown, press E again to harvest.
  4. Buy a new plot of land
    Walk to the edge of your island: light squares out at sea are for sale. Stand at the shore facing one and press E.
  5. Survive a night
    When the sky darkens, monsters wander out. Stay by your campfire, fight back when they come close, and live until dawn.
Reward
70 coins, 1 skill point, 6 Wheat Seeds, 4 Carrot Seeds

3. Hot Metal

Chapter 3 of 16

Stone, fire and patience: your first metal tools.

  1. Build a Furnace
    Press B, open the Industry tab, pick the Furnace (12 stone) and place it. Break boulders for stone.
  2. Smelt 6 iron bars
    Mine iron ore (the dark metal nodes), then press E at the Furnace, put the ore in and give it coal or wood as fuel. Collect the bars when done.
  3. Learn 3 skills (K)
    Press K. Every level gives a skill point: click a glowing skill next to the hub to learn it.
  4. Reach level 6
    Keep chopping, mining, crafting and fighting: it all earns XP.
  5. Break 8 boulders
    Hold Space next to a grey boulder. Stone flies to you; a better pick is faster.
Reward
100 coins, 2 skill points, 6 Iron Bar, 10 Plank

4. Reaching Out

Chapter 4 of 16

Your friends are somewhere out there. Buy land towards them, and trade along the way.

  1. Own 6 plots
    Keep raising new land: stand at the shore facing a light square and press E. Prices go up with every plot you buy.
  2. Build a Market Stall
    Press B, open the Special tab, pick the Market Stall (6 wood, 4 stone) and place it.
  3. Earn 150 coins selling goods
    Stand next to your Market Stall and press E to sell things from your pockets. Crops, planks and bars sell well.
  4. Find another farmer: join your islands together (optional)
    Buy land towards another player's island (the minimap shows where they are) until your plots touch.
  5. Catch 3 fish (a rod is one workbench away) (optional)
    Craft a Fishing Rod at the Workbench (C). Face open water and press Q to cast; press it again when the line bites.
Reward
150 coins, 2 skill points, 1 Swift Charm

5. Night Watch

Chapter 5 of 16

The monsters get bolder. Gear up and learn to fight.

  1. Defeat 12 monsters
    Hold Space next to a monster to hit it. A sword from the Workbench or Anvil hits much harder than a pick.
  2. Wear body armor
    Craft any body armor (iron mail needs the Anvil and iron bars), then open the Backpack (I) and click it to put it on.
  3. Survive 3 nights
    Stay near a campfire or lantern after dark, and keep a healing potion handy. Sunrise counts the night as survived.
  4. Build 2 lanterns
    A Lantern is 2 planks and 1 glass (melt sand in a Furnace to get glass). Press B, open the Light tab and place two.
  5. Reach level 10
    Fighting at night is the quickest XP, but everything you do adds up.
Reward
200 coins, 2 skill points, 2 Ironhide Tonic, 2 Might Draught

6. Wild Hearts

Chapter 6 of 16

Not everything out here wants to hurt you. Make friends and put them to work.

  1. Learn Pod Crafter in the Taming branch
    Press K and open the pink Taming branch: Pod Crafter is the first skill. It needs one skill point.
  2. Craft 3 Taming Pods
    Press C and craft Taming Pods (you need the Pod Crafter skill, and the materials listed in the recipe).
  3. Befriend a creature (T)
    Creatures are shy and rare: look around owned land, walk up slowly and press T to throw a pod. Stronger pods catch better.
  4. Build a Creature Den
    Press B, open the Special tab and pick the Creature Den (30 wood, 10 planks, 4 rope).
  5. Put a creature to work
    Stand next to the Den and press E, then click a creature from your roster to move it in. Or press P, pick a creature and choose Give a job… to put it on an island or at a machine.
Reward
200 coins, 2 skill points, 4 Taming Pod, 4 Creature Treat

7. Gears and Grids

Chapter 7 of 16

Why carry things yourself? Build a machine that does it.

  1. Build a Mining Drill
    Learn Mining Machines (K, Industry). Put the 2×2 Drill on coloured ore, with power and a belt or chest on the tile in front. V, Starter layouts has a ready-made Mining outpost.
  2. Lay 10 belts
    Learn Logistics (K). Pick the Conveyor Belt in Build, drag to lay a line from the drill to a chest and press R to turn a piece. The arrows on a belt show its way. 1 iron bar each.
  3. Build a Power Pole and a Wind Turbine
    Learn Electricity (K). Place a Wind Turbine, then a Power Pole within 3 tiles of it and of your machines. A red bolt over a machine means it has no power yet.
  4. Place an Inserter
    Learn Logistics (K). An Inserter picks up from the tile behind it and drops on the tile in front: chest to furnace, furnace to chest. It needs power. The Smelter line in V is a working one.
  5. Make 40 iron bars with machines
    Put iron ore and coal in the first chest of the Smelter line (V, Starter layouts), or feed a Furnace by hand. Bars made by machines count. Hover a machine to see what it is waiting for.
Reward
300 coins, 2 skill points, 4 Circuit, 8 Gear

8. The First Challenge

Chapter 8 of 16

An altar, a sigil and a very large slime. Bring friends if you can.

  1. Build a Boss Altar
    Learn Boss Hunter in the Combat branch (K), then build the Altar (stone, bricks, iron bars) from the Special tab.
  2. Craft a Slime Sigil
    Press E at the Altar and craft the sigil from slime gel and other drops. Slimes are everywhere at night.
  3. Defeat the Slime King
    Use the sigil at the Altar (E). Dodge the red warnings on the ground, dash with Shift, bring potions and friends.
Reward
300 coins, 3 skill points, 4 Healing Potion, 3 Gold Bar

9. Deeper

Chapter 9 of 16

Steel, crystal and stronger company.

  1. Reach level 18
    Bosses, expeditions and night fights give the most XP.
  2. Craft 4 steel
    Learn Steelwork in the Industry branch (K), then smelt iron bars and coal together in a Furnace.
  3. Defeat 60 monsters
    Fight at night near your farm, or clear waves on expeditions.
  4. Befriend 5 creatures
    Keep throwing pods (T) at the wild creatures you meet. Rare ones take better pods.
  5. Own 20 plots
    Keep raising land. Biomes further out hold richer ore and new creatures.
Reward
400 coins, 3 skill points, 4 Crystal Shard, 3 Swift Brew

10. The Great Work

Chapter 10 of 16

A farm that runs itself, and a wonder to prove it.

  1. Build an Assembler
    Learn Assembly (K, Industry). The Assembler needs power (a pole and a turbine) and builds parts from what you feed it. V, Starter layouts has a Gear cell to place.
  2. Make 20 circuits
    Open the Assembler (E) and choose Circuit, then feed it iron bars, wire and glass by hand or with inserters. A yellow hourglass over it means it is waiting: hover to see for what.
  3. Have 3 creatures working
    Press P, pick a creature and choose Give a job… (an island, a furnace, a workbench), or move creatures into a Creature Den (E at it). Three at work in all.
  4. Raise the Golden Windmill
    Press B, open the Special tab: the Golden Windmill is a wonder that costs a lot of everything.
Reward
600 coins, 3 skill points, 1 Power Core, 2 Motor

11. Four Corners

Chapter 11 of 16

The other guardians of the island are waking up.

  1. Defeat 4 different bosses
    Every biome has a guardian: Stone Colossus (quarry), Bog Witch (bog), Dune Pharaoh (desert), Frost Giant (snow). Their sigils need drops from monsters of that biome.
  2. Reach level 30
    Bosses and expeditions give lots of XP.
  3. Befriend a Rare creature
    Rare creatures are shy. The Rare Finder skill in the Taming branch makes them show up more; use a Great Pod.
Reward
1000 coins, 4 skill points, 8 Gold Bar, 8 Crystal Shard

12. Into the Rift

Chapter 12 of 16

There are islands at the edge of the sea that were not there yesterday. A dock, a few friends and a plan.

  1. Build an Expedition Dock
    Learn Expedition in the Explore branch (K), then build the Dock by the water from the Special tab.
  2. Clear the Mossy Rift
    Press E at the Dock and launch the first tier. Survive every wave and beat the guardian.
  3. Clear 15 expedition waves
    Every wave you clear on any expedition counts, even if the run is lost later.
  4. Pick 6 boons on expeditions (optional)
    Between waves you choose one of three boons. Each pick counts.
Reward
800 coins, 3 skill points, 12 Rift Shard, 4 Crystal Shard

13. The Old Heart

Chapter 13 of 16

Every island leads to the centre. Something there has been waiting for you all.

  1. Own the centre plot
    Buy land towards the red-outlined plot in the middle of the world map (Esc, then World Map).
  2. Defeat all five guardians
    Slime King, Stone Colossus, Bog Witch, Dune Pharaoh and Frost Giant.
  3. Defeat the Old Heart
    Build an Altar on the centre plot and wake it with the Heart Sigil. Bring everyone, and your best gear.
Reward
3000 coins, 6 skill points, 1 Heartstone

14. A Stranger on the Pier

Chapter 14 of 16

Somebody new has stepped off a boat with a very large wheel. Madame Fortuna says luck is something you can build.

  1. Build the Fortune Wheel
    Press B, open the Special tab and pick the Fortune Wheel (20 planks, 4 cloth, 2 gold bars and 60 coins). It is 2 by 2 tiles: place it on free ground.
  2. Spin the wheel 5 times
    Walk up to the Fortune Wheel and press E. Your first spin each day is free; after that a spin costs coins, a little more each time.
  3. Open 3 crates
    Crates come from the wheel, from monsters and from bosses. Open the Backpack (I) and click a crate to open it.
  4. Find 3 Lucky Finds
    Every time you chop, mine or pick something there is a small chance of a Lucky Find: a bonus pops out. Keep gathering. A Lucky Charm and luck skills (K) help.
Reward
1200 coins, 3 skill points, 2 Silver Crate, 3 Healing Potion

15. X Marks the Spot

Chapter 15 of 16

Lumpy mounds in the ground and letters in bottles all point the same way. Somebody has been leaving clues for a very long time.

  1. Dig up 5 buried treasures
    Now and then, usually at dawn, a lumpy mound of earth appears on land you own. Hold Space next to it with your pick to dig it up. Bottles can point you to one.
  2. Read 2 messages in bottles
    Bottles come up on your fishing line now and then (Q to cast), and crates can hold them. Open the Backpack (I) and click a bottle to read it.
  3. Break a golden node
    Now and then a tree or rock grows with a golden shine. Hold Space next to it: it gives much more than usual, plus a prize on top.
  4. Open 10 crates
    Spin the Fortune Wheel (E) for crates. Monsters and bosses drop them too. Open each one from the Backpack (I) by clicking it.
Reward
2000 coins, 3 skill points, 1 Golden Crate, 1 Lantern Shard

16. The Last Lantern

Chapter 16 of 16

The wheel was never about luck. It is the heart of an old lantern, and it is hungry for light.

  1. Find 3 Lantern Shards
    Lantern Shards come from Golden and Mythic Crates, wheel jackpots, golden nodes and rare buried treasure. Check the Backpack (I). The lantern needs 3 of them to be made.
  2. Craft Fortune’s Lantern
    Stand at a Workbench and press C. It takes 3 Lantern Shards, 4 gold bars, 4 crystal shards and 1 pearl. Then click it in the Backpack (I) to wear it.
  3. Reach level 35
    Bosses, expeditions and night fights give the most XP, and everything else adds up.
  4. Win a wheel jackpot (optional)
    Spin the Fortune Wheel (E). The JACKPOT wedge is tiny, but its odds grow with every paid spin that misses it.
  5. Win 3 double-or-nothings (optional)
    After a coin prize on the wheel, press Double or nothing instead of taking it. You win a little less than half the time, so be patient.
Reward
5000 coins, 8 skill points, 2 Mythic Crate

Side quests (49, from 9 people)

Marta Greenthumb Gardener

First Sprouts

from level 2

"Soil is no good sitting empty, dear. Pop a few seeds into a garden bed and see what comes up. I will show you the rest."

  1. Plant 4 seeds
    Break wildflowers and bushes for seeds. Build a Garden Bed (B), pick a seed on your hotbar or just walk up and press E to plant.
  2. Harvest 4 crops
    Wait until the plant looks fully grown, then press E on the bed again. Wheat is the quickest.
Reward
40 coins, 30 XP, 4 Carrot Seeds

Green Acres

from level 3, after First Sprouts

"One bed is a hobby, five is a farm. Put in a proper patch and bring me ten good wheat for the winter stores."

  1. Build 3 more Garden Beds
    Press B, open the Farm tab, pick the Garden Bed (3 wood) and place three more in a row.
  2. Harvest 12 crops
    Keep every bed planted: harvest with E, replant straight away.
  3. Hold 10 wheat
    Wheat seeds come from wildflowers. Wheat grows quickest of all the crops.
Reward
90 coins, 60 XP, 3 Pumpkin Seeds
After
First Sprouts

Daily Bread

from level 5, after Green Acres

"Wheat on its own never filled a belly. Grind it, and we will talk about bread."

  1. Build a Millstone
    Press B, open the Industry tab and pick the Millstone (14 stone, 4 planks).
  2. Grind 10 flour
    Press E at the Millstone, put wheat in, and collect the flour. Machines you built count, even when a creature carries for you.
Reward
120 coins, 80 XP, 4 Bread
After
Green Acres

Pretty as a Picture

from level 7, after Daily Bread

"A farm should look as good as it works. A few planters, a fence to keep the chickens honest, and somewhere to sit."

  1. Build 2 Flower Planters
    Press B, open the Decor tab: Flower Planter (2 planks, 2 fiber).
  2. Build 8 Wooden Fences
    Decor tab again: a fence is 1 plank. Click and drag to lay a few at once.
  3. Build a Bench
    Decor tab: Bench (3 planks).
Reward
100 coins, 70 XP, 3 Melon Seeds
After
Daily Bread

A Light in the Garden

from level 8, after Daily Bread

"My gran swore flowers lean towards a lantern, the way people lean towards a good story. Put two lights beside your beds and plant a dozen seeds."

  1. Build 2 Lanterns
    Press B, open the Light tab and place two Lanterns (2 planks and 1 glass each). Melt sand in a Furnace to get glass.
  2. Plant 12 seeds
    Break wildflowers and bushes for seeds, then walk up to a Garden Bed and press E to plant. Replant every time you harvest.
Reward
110 coins, 80 XP, 1 Wooden Crate, 4 Pumpkin Seeds
After
Daily Bread

A Spoonful of Luck

from level 10, after Daily Bread

"Berry jam, dear. A spoonful in the morning and the whole day goes your way, or so my gran said, and she won more raffles than anybody. Build a kitchen and let us find out."

  1. Build a Kitchen
    Learn Kitchen Know-How in the Farming and Cooking branch (K). Then press B, open the Crafting tab: the Kitchen is 4 bricks, 4 planks and 6 stone.
  2. Cook 4 Berry Jam
    Press C at the Kitchen: Berry Jam is 6 berries, so pick plenty of berry bushes. Eating jam gives you a short Lucky buff.
Reward
140 coins, 90 XP, 2 Wooden Crate
After
Daily Bread

Odo Travelling trader

Open for Business

from level 3

"Friend, a farm that cannot sell is a hobby. Put up a stall and show me you can part with something."

  1. Build a Market Stall
    Press B, open the Special tab and pick the Market Stall (6 wood, 4 stone).
  2. Sell goods for 100 coins
    Stand next to the stall and press E. Crops, planks and bars all sell; the stall shows the price.
Reward
60 coins, 40 XP

Supply and Demand

from level 5, after Open for Business

"Everybody wants planks and nobody wants to saw them. Stock up and I will make it worth your while."

  1. Craft 20 planks
    Stand at a Workbench (C). A Sawmill turns logs into twice as many planks.
  2. Hold 40 wood
    Chop trees (hold Space next to them). Trees grow back.
  3. Earn 250 coins selling goods
    Sell at your Market Stall (E).
Reward
140 coins, 80 XP, 1 Gold Bar
After
Open for Business

Shop Around

from level 8, after Supply and Demand

"I only visit markets that have shown they can pay. Learn my contacts and see what I carry that you cannot make."

  1. Buy 3 things from the Trader
    Learn Merchant Contacts in the Explore branch (K). Then stand next to a Market Stall and open the trader (E) to buy from his cart.
Reward
160 coins, 100 XP, 1 skill point
After
Supply and Demand

Pockets Full of Coin

from level 14, after Shop Around

"A real merchant counts in thousands. Show me you are ready for the big leagues."

  1. Earn 1500 coins selling goods
    Sell what your machines make. Steel, circuits and gears fetch the best prices.
Reward
400 coins, 200 XP, 1 skill point
After
Shop Around

A Map for a Song

from level 12, after Shop Around

"I picked up a map in the south, friend. Odd thing: it shows your farm. Spend a little at my cart, earn a little at your stall, and it is yours for a song."

  1. Buy 2 things from the Trader
    Stand next to a Market Stall and open the trader (E) to buy from his cart. You need Merchant Contacts in the Explore branch (K).
  2. Earn 400 coins selling goods
    Sell crops, planks and bars at your Market Stall (E). The stall shows the price.
Reward
200 coins, 130 XP, 1 Silver Crate
After
Shop Around

Land Rush

from level 10, after Supply and Demand

"Land is the one thing they stopped making, friend, which is why the price keeps climbing. Buy a few plots before it does, and sell what grows on them."

  1. Buy 3 new plots of land
    Walk to the shore of your island, face a light square out at sea and press E. Each plot costs a little more than the last.
  2. Earn 600 coins selling goods
    Sell what your land grows at a Market Stall (E): crops, planks and bars all fetch a fair price.
Reward
180 coins, 110 XP, 2 Wooden Crate
After
Supply and Demand

Pip Creature keeper

A Friend in Need

from level 4

"Shh, do not scare them. Creatures here are not monsters, they are neighbours who have not met you yet. Make a few pods and say hello."

  1. Learn Pod Crafter
    Press K and open the pink Taming branch: Pod Crafter is the first skill and needs one skill point.
  2. Craft 2 Taming Pods
    Press C at a Workbench: pods are planks, fiber and slime gel.
  3. Befriend a creature
    Creatures wander owned land, rarely. Walk up slowly and press T to throw a pod; they can break free, so bring spares.
Reward
60 coins, 50 XP, 4 Taming Pod, 3 Creature Treat

Lend a Paw

from level 5, after A Friend in Need

"Your friend would rather be busy than bored. Take them along (P, then Take along) and give them a job: they will chop, mine or tend the crops beside you."

  1. Give your companion a task
    Open the Creatures screen (P), pick the creature you took along and choose a task: Gather, Mine, Farm or Guard. Or tap the task button on the companion card at the top left.
  2. Let it work 15 times
    While it has a task your companion goes off to chop, mine or tend crops near you. Stay close and keep working: you will see little work puffs.
Reward
100 coins, 80 XP, 5 Creature Treat
After
A Friend in Need

Home Sweet Den

from level 6, after A Friend in Need

"Not everyone wants to walk all day. Build them a den and they will keep house, gather and haul without ever being asked twice."

  1. Build a Creature Den
    Press B, open the Special tab: the Den costs 30 wood, 10 planks and 4 rope.
  2. Have 2 creatures working
    Stand next to the Den and press E, then click creatures from your roster to move them in. Each has its own strengths. (Creatures with a job from P count too.)
Reward
150 coins, 100 XP, 1 skill point
After
A Friend in Need

Training Day

from level 8, after Lend a Paw

"A little love and a lot of treats make a champion. Raise one of yours, and find a few friends for it."

  1. Raise a creature to level 6
    Creatures earn XP when they fight beside you or work in a den. Treats in the Creatures screen (P) speed it up.
  2. Befriend 3 more creatures
    Keep throwing pods at the wild ones you find (T).
Reward
160 coins, 120 XP, 2 Greater Pod, 6 Creature Treat
After
Lend a Paw

The Egg Problem

from level 14, after Home Sweet Den

"Two creatures, a quiet corner and a few treats. What could go wrong? Build a hatchery and find out."

  1. Build a Hatchery
    Learn Matchmaker in the Taming branch (K). Press B, Special tab: the Hatchery needs planks, bricks, rope and a gold bar.
  2. Hatch an egg
    Press E at the Hatchery, pick two free creatures and bring a few treats. When the egg is ready, press E to hatch it.
Reward
300 coins, 200 XP, 1 skill point
After
Home Sweet Den

A Proper Job

from level 7, after Lend a Paw

"Walking beside you is lovely, but I have seen what they can do on their own. Give one an island to look after and another a machine to run, then go and have lunch."

  1. Put a creature on an island
    Place a Chest near the middle of an island you own. Then press P, pick a creature, choose Give a job… and click the island and a job (Lumber, Mining, Farming, Hauling or Guarding).
  2. Put a creature at a machine
    Open a Furnace, Sawmill, Millstone or Workbench (E) and press Put a creature to work. Keep a Chest within nine tiles with what it needs.
  3. Let your creatures work 30 times
    Leave them to it and go and do something else: every harvest, felled tree or finished item counts. The bubble over a worker says if it needs help.
Reward
220 coins, 150 XP, 1 skill point, 6 Creature Treat
After
Lend a Paw

Friends of the Light

from level 10, after A Friend in Need

"Creatures drift towards a warm light at dusk. The old ones even walk the same route every night, as if they still remember where it leads. Put up some lanterns and see who comes."

  1. Build 3 Lanterns
    Press B, open the Light tab and place three Lanterns (2 planks and 1 glass each) near the edge of your land.
  2. Befriend a creature
    Creatures wander owned land, rarely. Walk up slowly and press T to throw a pod; they can break free, so bring spares.
Reward
130 coins, 100 XP, 1 Greater Pod, 1 Wooden Crate
After
A Friend in Need

Captain Brine Old sailor

First Cast

from level 3

"There is nothing like the sea for thinking. Or not thinking. Cast a line and let the water do the talking."

  1. Catch 3 fish
    Craft a Fishing Rod at the Workbench (C). Face open water and press Q to cast; press it again when the line bites.
  2. Catch 2 kinds of fish
    Different fish bite in different biomes and at different times of day.
Reward
50 coins, 40 XP

Bigger Fish

from level 6, after First Cast

"Small fry feed the cat. If you want to impress me, bring home variety."

  1. Catch 12 fish
    A better rod brings quicker bites and bigger fish.
  2. Catch 5 kinds of fish
    Try fishing off the bog, the snow and the desert coasts at night as well as by day.
Reward
120 coins, 90 XP
After
First Cast

Pearls Before Swine

from level 10, after Bigger Fish

"Once in a blue moon the sea gives something back. Keep casting and keep your eyes open."

  1. Fish up a pearl
    Pearls are rare catches. A better rod and the Angler skill help; keep fishing.
Reward
200 coins, 140 XP, 1 skill point
After
Bigger Fish

Rift of the Day

from level 16, after Pearls Before Swine

"There is a new rift every day and a lot of fools who skip it. Show me you are not one of them."

  1. Clear the rift of the day
    Build an Expedition Dock (Explore branch), then launch the Rift of the Day from it (E). Once a day.
Reward
300 coins, 200 XP, 1 skill point
After
Pearls Before Swine

The Fish That Glows

from level 9, after Bigger Fish

"When I was small there were lights on the water, and the lanternfish followed them like ducklings. These days you hardly see one. Go out after dark and catch me one, would you?"

  1. Catch a Lanternfish
    Lanternfish only bite after dark. Cast off any shore at night (Q) and be patient; bait helps, and so does a better rod.
Reward
180 coins, 130 XP, 1 Silver Crate
After
Bigger Fish

Ferro Smith and tinkerer

Hot Stuff

from level 5

"Iron does not forge itself, though I wish it did. Light the furnace and make something that bites."

  1. Smelt 10 iron bars
    Mine iron ore, then press E at a Furnace and give it ore and coal or wood.
  2. Craft an iron pick
    Learn Smithing in the Industry branch (K), build an Anvil, then press C next to it.
Reward
100 coins, 80 XP

Bright Ideas

from level 7, after Hot Stuff

"I tripped over my own anvil again. Light the place up, would you?"

  1. Build 3 lanterns
    A Lantern is 2 planks and 1 glass. Melt sand in a Furnace for glass.
  2. Build a Lamp Post
    Press B, open the Light tab: the Lamp Post is 2 iron bars and 1 glass.
Reward
90 coins, 70 XP, 6 Glass
After
Hot Stuff

Mind the Stockpile

from level 10, after Hot Stuff

"A smith without stock is just a man with a hammer. Build a proper storeroom and fill it."

  1. Build 2 Chests
    Press B, Storage tab: a Chest is 8 wood and holds 400 items.
  2. Hold 200 stone
    Break boulders; a better pick breaks them faster. Wear a pack (Backpack, I) to carry more of every item.
Reward
150 coins, 100 XP, 20 Plank
After
Hot Stuff

Let There Be Power

from level 14, after Mind the Stockpile

"Muscle is for amateurs. Wind is free and never complains."

  1. Build a Wind Turbine
    Learn Power in the Industry branch (K). The Wind Turbine is in the Power tab of the build menu.
  2. Place 4 Power Poles
    A Power Pole wires machines within 3 tiles and links to poles within 7.
Reward
250 coins, 160 XP, 1 skill point
After
Mind the Stockpile

A Roof Over Your Head

from level 6

"Out in the open the night finds you. Put walls round yourself, leave one way in, and a roof on top. Doors keep the monsters out."

  1. Lay 6 plank floors
    Press B, open the Floors & walls tab and pick the Plank Floor (1 plank). Drag across the ground to lay a row.
  2. Raise 8 wooden walls
    In the same Floors & walls tab, the Wooden Wall is 2 planks; drag along the edge of the floor and the pieces join up.
  3. Build a Doorway
    Leave a gap in the wall and put a Doorway in it. You can walk through; monsters cannot. R turns it.
  4. Put on 6 thatch roofs
    The Thatch Roof is 3 fiber and 1 plank. Drag it across the floor. It fades when you stand under it.
Reward
140 coins, 90 XP, 1 Satchel, 10 Plank

Room for More

from level 8, after A Roof Over Your Head

"A satchel is a start. A proper rucksack of hide and rope carries a hundred more of everything. Make one."

  1. Craft a Rucksack
    You need 6 hide (Tuskers drop it), 6 rope (2 fiber each, by hand) and 4 planks. Craft it at a Workbench with C.
  2. Wear a pack
    A pack you craft is put on for you if your bag slot is empty; otherwise open the Backpack (I) and click it. Your carry limit is shown at the bottom.
Reward
160 coins, 100 XP, 10 Rope
After
A Roof Over Your Head

Odd Glass

from level 12, after Bright Ideas, story chapter 11

"That scrap of glass from the foundations is bothering me. I cannot melt it or break it, and it hums at dusk. Make me some honest glass to compare it with, and two lamp posts to hold it next to."

  1. Melt 12 glass
    Put sand and fuel into a Furnace (E): it melts into glass. Machines you built count, even when a creature feeds them.
  2. Build 2 Lamp Posts
    Press B, open the Light tab: a Lamp Post is 2 iron bars and 1 glass.
Reward
220 coins, 140 XP, 2 Wooden Crate
After
Bright Ideas

The Night Watcher Keeper of the dark

The Long Dark

from level 2

"The dark is not your enemy. It is a test. Stay by the fire, strike what comes close, and see the sun again."

  1. Survive 2 nights
    Stay near a campfire or lantern after dark. Dawn counts the night as survived.
  2. Defeat 6 monsters
    Hold Space next to a monster to hit it. A sword helps a lot.
Reward
50 coins, 50 XP, 2 Healing Potion

Gel for Ghosts

from level 4, after The Long Dark

"Slimes are the first thing the dark learns to make. Their gel is good for many things. Bring me some."

  1. Defeat 10 slimes
    Slimes wander out at night and hop towards you. Two hits each.
  2. Hold 8 slime gel
    Slimes drop gel most of the time. Gel is also used for pods.
Reward
80 coins, 70 XP, 1 Might Draught
After
The Long Dark

Bones of the Night

from level 8, after Gel for Ghosts

"Not everything that walks at night is soft. The bones will test you."

  1. Defeat 5 skeletons
    They rattle out of the dark and walk straight at you. Keep moving and swing when they are close.
  2. Defeat 3 bone archers
    Archers keep their distance and shoot. Close in fast, or fight them around a corner.
Reward
160 coins, 120 XP, 1 skill point
After
Gel for Ghosts

Elite Hunter

from level 12, after Bones of the Night

"Some of them are older and meaner than the rest. They glow. Put three of them down."

  1. Defeat 3 elite monsters
    Elites glow and have more health. They begin to show up from level 6, mostly at night.
Reward
300 coins, 200 XP, 1 skill point, 2 Ironhide Tonic
After
Bones of the Night

Keep the Lights On

from level 9, after Gel for Ghosts

"Once there was a light on every island, kept burning all night. Then there was not. I cannot light them all, but you can light a few. Four lanterns, and three nights to prove they stay lit."

  1. Build 4 Lanterns
    Press B, open the Light tab: a Lantern is 2 planks and 1 glass. Melt sand in a Furnace to get glass.
  2. Survive 3 nights
    Stay near your lanterns or a campfire after dark. Dawn counts the night as survived.
Reward
130 coins, 100 XP, 1 Ironhide Tonic, 1 Wooden Crate
After
Gel for Ghosts

Lost in the Dark

from level 18, after Elite Hunter

"Wraiths are what is left when a light goes out and nobody notices. Cold, lost things. Lay four of them to rest, and bring me what they leave behind."

  1. Defeat 4 wraiths
    Wraiths drift through the dark and hurl cold bolts. Fight near a lantern or campfire, keep moving, and bring healing potions.
  2. Hold 2 Wraith Shrouds
    Wraiths often drop a Wraith Shroud when they fall. Check your Backpack (I).
Reward
320 coins, 190 XP, 1 skill point, 1 Silver Crate
After
Elite Hunter

Madame Fortuna Fortune teller

A Spin of Fortune

from level 8

"Darling, you have the look of someone who enjoys a little surprise. Build me a wheel and give it one spin. The first spin of every day is on the house."

  1. Build a Fortune Wheel
    Press B, open the Special tab and pick the Fortune Wheel (20 planks, 4 cloth, 2 gold bars and 60 coins). It is 2 by 2 tiles.
  2. Spin the wheel once
    Walk up to the Fortune Wheel and press E, then spin it. Your first spin each day is free.
Reward
120 coins, 90 XP, 1 Wooden Crate, 1 Silver Crate

Press Your Luck

after A Spin of Fortune

"A wise farmer takes the winnings. A fun farmer takes a risk. Win a coin prize on the wheel, then tell me you will double it. Twice."

  1. Try double or nothing 2 times
    Win a coin prize on the wheel (the half, 1x, 2x, 5x or 12x wedges), then press Double or nothing instead of taking it. Win or lose, each try counts.
Reward
160 coins, 110 XP, 2 Wooden Crate
After
A Spin of Fortune

The Crate Whisperer

after A Spin of Fortune

"Crates, darling. They rattle, they gleam, they keep secrets. Open five, and make sure one of them has silver bands. Listen to the lock. It talks."

  1. Open 5 crates
    Crates come from the Fortune Wheel, from monsters and from bosses. Open the Backpack (I) and click a crate to open it.
  2. Open a Silver Crate
    Silver Crates come from the wheel’s Silver wedge and from tougher monsters, and some quests pay them. Click one in the Backpack (I).
Reward
200 coins, 130 XP, 1 Silver Crate
After
A Spin of Fortune

Lucky Streak

from level 10, after Press Your Luck

"Luck is a habit, darling. Chop, mine, pick, and let the world surprise you. Five little surprises, and one golden one if the sky is feeling generous."

  1. Find 5 Lucky Finds
    Every time you chop, mine or pick something there is a small chance of a Lucky Find: a bonus pops out. A Lucky Charm and luck skills (K) help.
  2. Break a golden node
    Now and then a tree or rock grows with a golden shine. Hold Space next to it: it gives much more, plus a prize.
Reward
260 coins, 150 XP, 1 skill point, 3 Wooden Crate
After
Press Your Luck

Lady Luck’s Favourite

from level 14, after Lucky Streak

"The wheel has a favourite, you know. It will never admit it, but it does a little wiggle when you walk up. Spin it twenty-five times and win two gambles, and we shall see who blinks."

  1. Spin the wheel 25 times
    Press E at the Fortune Wheel. One spin a day is free; the others cost coins, a little more each time, so spread them over a few days.
  2. Win 2 double-or-nothings
    After a coin prize on the wheel, press Double or nothing instead of taking it. You win a little less than half the time, so be patient.
Reward
400 coins, 200 XP, 1 skill point, 1 Golden Crate
After
Lucky Streak

Finn Digsby Treasure hunter

Dig Deeper

from level 7

"Psst, you. See the lumpy bits of ground that were not there yesterday? Not rocks, not molehills. Opportunities. Dig up two and I will show you the best day of your life. A little muddy."

  1. Dig up 2 buried treasures
    Now and then, usually at dawn, a lumpy mound of earth appears on land you own. Hold Space next to it with your pick to dig it up.
Reward
100 coins, 80 XP, 1 Wooden Crate

A Letter from the Sea

after Dig Deeper

"The sea buries things too, you know. Cast a line, catch a few fish, and if the water is kind it will send a letter back. Read the first one you find and tell me what it says."

  1. Catch 8 fish
    Craft a Fishing Rod at the Workbench (C). Face open water and press Q to cast; press it again when the line bites.
  2. Read a message in a bottle
    Now and then a bottle comes up on your line, and crates can hold one too. Open the Backpack (I) and click the bottle to read it.
Reward
160 coins, 110 XP, 2 Wooden Crate
After
Dig Deeper

X Marks the Spot

from level 12, after A Letter from the Sea

"Six holes and three letters. That is how a Digsby finds a fortune, and also how a Digsby finds a lot of holes. Dig, read, and let us see if the map makes sense."

  1. Dig up 6 buried treasures
    Lumpy mounds appear on land you own now and then, usually at dawn. Hold Space next to one with your pick. Some bottles point you to one.
  2. Read 3 messages in bottles
    Bottles come up on your fishing line now and then, and crates can hold them. Open the Backpack (I) and click a bottle to read it.
Reward
260 coins, 160 XP, 1 skill point, 1 Silver Crate
After
A Letter from the Sea

The Lanternkeepers

from level 18, after X Marks the Spot

"The big one. The letters talk of lantern glass, shards that glow when nobody is looking. Find two, plus a few rare treasures, so we know the keepers left us something worth the trouble."

  1. Hold 2 Lantern Shards
    Lantern Shards come from Golden and Mythic Crates, wheel jackpots, golden nodes and rare buried treasure. Check the Backpack (I).
  2. Get 3 rare finds
    An Epic or Legendary item from a crate, the wheel or a buried treasure counts. Golden and Mythic crates hold the most.
Reward
400 coins, 200 XP, 1 skill point, 1 Golden Crate
After
X Marks the Spot

Rocco Underhill Miner

Down the Shaft

from level 8

"There is a whole second world under your boots, friend, and nobody mining it. Sink a shaft, climb down and break some rock. Bring a lantern: it is blacker than a bog witch's hat down there."

  1. Climb down a mine shaft
    Learn Mine Shafts (K, Gathering branch), build a Mine Shaft (B, Special tab) and press E on it.
  2. Dig 25 pieces of rock
    Hold the action key or your finger beside rock in the caves.
Reward
60 coins, 60 XP, 20 Stone

Ore in the Dark

from level 9, after Down the Shaft

"Plain rock is for walls. The money is in the veins: coal for the furnace, iron and copper for everything else, and the gold glints when you are close. Dig me some."

  1. Dig 8 iron ore
    Iron shows as rusty speckles in the rock. Hold the action key beside it.
  2. Dig 4 gold ore
    Gold glints yellow, and is commoner towards the edge of the map.
Reward
90 coins, 90 XP, 3 Healing Potion
After
Down the Shaft

What the Dark Keeps

from level 11, after Ore in the Dark

"Folk have been leaving things down there since before the islands rose. Chests, mostly. And the crystal: it needs a golden pick or better, mind."

  1. Open 3 treasure chests
    Treasure chests stand in the open caves. Walk up and hit one.
  2. Dig 3 crystal
    Crystal clusters need a gold pick (Smithing 2). Look for the blue glow in the rock.
Reward
140 coins, 150 XP, 1 Silver Crate
After
Ore in the Dark

Luck and loot

Crates open from the Backpack (click one). Better crates roll more things, bigger stacks and rarer classes. All the luck dice are separate from the world's, so luck never changes how the world grows.

4% of every node you break (more with luck): coins, something useful, now and then a crate

each owned island has a 30% chance each dawn of a mound with a red X; dig it like a rock

Pity
silver crates and better: after 8 without an Epic, the next one holds one

Multiplayer and servers

Up to sixteen farmers share one world. Everybody starts on their own island, far apart, and buys land towards the others until the islands connect. Chests, machines and the Market Stall are shared.

Join a server from the title screen: paste its address in the Join box (a link with ?server= fills it in for you), pick a name and a secret word. Use the same name and word on any phone or computer and you are the same farmer. Solo worlds live in your browser.

The always-on world runs on a server around the clock, so the farm keeps going (machines, creatures at work, the seasons) while everybody is away. Anyone can also host a world on a PC with the server package.

A world on a server runs whether anybody is online or not. Creatures keep working, machines keep running, and your farmer waits where you left them.

Playing together

  • Up to 16 farmers per world. Farmers within 800 px count as a party for how hard the night is.
  • A downed friend is picked up by holding E beside them for 2.5 s.
  • Chat with Enter, emotes with G then 1 to 8, ping a spot with the middle mouse button (or Alt+click).
  • Waystones (Explorer: Waystones) let everybody step between them.
  • The Journal's Chronicle tab is the story of your farm, written as it happens and shared by everybody.

The evening hearth

In the dusk countdown, farmers at a campfire, table, lantern or lamp post sit at it; your own companion nearby counts as company. Two heads make a circle: the fire fills and kindles, and everybody sitting there gets Hearthside until dawn.

Where
Campfire, Table, Lantern, Lamp Post
Kindling
5 s of sitting together in the dusk countdown
Hearthside
+0.8 Energy regen / s +25% Healing power +15% XP gain, a heart every 8 s, until dawn

The potluck table

A Table holds dishes that friends set out. Pressing E at it eats a portion of every dish that helps you, each buff marked with its cook.

Dishes
4 different, 8 portions each
Feasting
once every 20 s per farmer, within 3 tiles

Mailbox and postcards

A Mailbox belongs to its builder. At a friend's mailbox you can send a parcel (a few kinds of things, coins count) with a note. Every dawn one of the island folk sends each farmer a postcard with a small present.

A parcel
up to 3 kinds of things and a note of up to 60 letters
Waiting post
up to 20 letters, 3 from the same friend
Postcards
the newest 3 are kept

The season wish

On the first morning of every season after the first, the farm is offered three wishes (the same three for everybody, from the world's seed). Everybody online votes; the winner blesses every farmer until the season ends.

See every wish.

Buffs

Buffs are short boosts from food, potions and events. Eating the same buff again refreshes it rather than stacking. Buff duration skills make them last longer.

The evening hearth: sit at a fire with company in the last minute of the day.
